[TOOL CALL run_python]
def blocks(grid, r0,c0, br=3,bc=3):
    H,W=grid.shape
    res=[]
    for i in range(r0,r0+br):
        row=[]
        for j in range(c0,c0+bc):
            row.append(tuple(grid[3*i:3*i+3,3*j:3*j+3].flatten()))
        res.append(row)
    return res

for idx,ex in enumerate(train):
    inp=np.array(ex['input']); out=np.array(ex['output'])
    print("=== ex",idx)
    # figure bbox
    nz=np.argwhere(inp!=8); r0,c0=nz.min(0)
    # assume figure 9x9, aligned to multiples of 3? check
    print("fig origin",r0,c0)
    B=blocks(out,r0//3*3, c0//3*3)  # hmm

[TOOL CALL run_python]
import numpy as np, itertools

def get_fig(inp):
    nz=np.argwhere(inp!=8); r0,c0=nz.min(0); return r0,c0, inp[r0:r0+9,c0:c0+9]

def dihedral(b):
    res=[]
    for k in range(4):
        r=np.rot90(b,k)
        res.append(r); res.append(np.fliplr(r))
    return res  # 8 variants

for idx,ex in enumerate(train):
    inp=np.array(ex['input']); out=np.array(ex['output'])
    r0,c0,F=get_fig(inp)
    print("=== ex",idx,"r0,c0",r0,c0,"shape",out.shape)
    # block grid anchored at (r0,c0)
    # enumerate block rows indices from r0: allowed cells r0+3*k .. for k negative too
    # We'll consider all block index offsets
    for bi in range(-3,4):
        for bj in range(-3,4):
            rr=r0+3*bi; cc=c0+3*bj
            if rr<0 or cc<0 or rr+3>out.shape[0] or cc+3>out.shape[1]: continue
            blk=out[rr:rr+3,cc:cc+3]
            if np.all(blk==8): 
                tag="EMPTY"
            else:
                found=None
                for fi in range(3):
                    for fj in range(3):
                        if fi==1 and fj==1: continue
                        fb=F[3*fi:3*fi+3,3*fj:3*fj+3]
                        for t,var in enumerate(dihedral(fb)):
                            if np.array_equal(var,blk):
                                found=(fi,fj,t); break
                        if found: break
                    if found: break
                tag=str(found)
            print(f" block({bi},{bj}) rows{rr}:{rr+3} cols{cc}:{cc+3} -> {tag}")
    print()

[TOOL CALL run_python]
import numpy as np
inp=np.array(train[0]['input']); out=np.array(train[0]['output'])
r0,c0,F=3,3,inp[3:12,3:12]
H,W=out.shape
# pad F by 8 so out-of-range neighborhoods exist? We'll just use exact 3x3 within F
def nb(grid,r,c,k=1):
    return grid[r-k:r+k+1,c-k:c+k+1]
# precompute F neighborhoods
Fnp=F
# For each out interior cell, compare neighborhood with F neighborhoods allowing the hole?
matches={}
for r in range(1,H-1):
    for c in range(1,W-1):
        o=nb(out,r,c)
        ms=[]
        for fr in range(1,8):
            for fc in range(1,8):
                if np.array_equal(nb(Fnp,fr,fc),o):
                    ms.append((fr,fc))
        matches[(r,c)]=ms
# print a compact map: for each cell list count and if unique
for r in range(H):
    row=""
    for c in range(W):
        if r==0 or c==0 or r==H-1 or c==W-1:
            row+=" . "
        else:
            ms=matches[(r,c)]
            if len(ms)==1:
                fr,fc=ms[0]
                row+=f"{fr}{fc} "
            else:
                row+="#%d "%len(ms)
    print(f"{r:2d}",row)

[TOOL CALL run_python]
import numpy as np
out=np.array(train[0]['output']); H,W=out.shape
def match_shift(g,dr,dc,flip=False):
    if flip:
        g=np.flipud(g) if dr!=0 else g
    # overlapping region
    r0=max(0,dr); r1=min(H,H+dr); c0=max(0,dc); c1=min(W,W+dc)
    a=g[r0:r1,c0:c1]; b=g[r0-dr:r1-dr,c0-dc:c1-dc]
    return np.mean(a==b), a.size
for dr in range(-9,10):
    for dc in range(-9,10):
        if dr==0 and dc==0: continue
        m,sz=match_shift(out,dr,dc)
        if m>0.99:
            print("pure translation",dr,dc,round(m,3))
print("---flips---")
for dr in range(-9,10):
    for dc in range(0,10):
        if dr==0 and dc==0: continue
        g=np.flipud(out)
        m,sz=match_shift(g,dr,dc)
        if m>0.99:
            print("flipud then shift",dr,dc,round(m,3))

[TOOL CALL run_python]
import numpy as np
inp=np.array(train[0]['input']); out=np.array(train[0]['output'])
F=np.array(train[0]['input'])[3:12,3:12].copy()
Ff=F.copy(); Ff[3:6,3:6]=9
Ff=np.array(Ff)
orients=[]
for k in range(4):
    r=np.rot90(Ff,k)
    orients.append(r); orients.append(np.fliplr(r))
def nb(g,r,c):
    return g[r-1:r+2,c-1:c+2]
H,W=out.shape
res={}
for r in range(1,H-1):
    for c in range(1,W-1):
        o=nb(out,r,c)
        ms=[]
        for oi,g in enumerate(orients):
            for fr in range(1,8):
                for fc in range(1,8):
                    if np.array_equal(nb(g,fr,fc),o):
                        ms.append((oi,fr-4,fc-4))  # offset from center
        res[(r,c)]=ms
# summarize: for each out cell print one representative and count
for r in range(H):
    line=""
    for c in range(W):
        if (r,c) in res and res[(r,c)]:
            # prefer unique
            ms=res[(r,c)]
            line+=f"{len(ms):2d}"
        else:
            line+=" ."
    print(f"{r:2d} {line}")

import numpy as np
inp=np.array(train[2]['input']); out=np.array(train[2]['output'])
r0,c0=5,6
F=inp[r0:r0+9,c0:c0+9]
blocksF={}
for i in range(3):
    for j in range(3):
        blocksF[(i,j)]=F[3*i:3*i+3,3*j:3*j+3]
names={0:'id',1:'lr',2:'r90',3:'r90lr',4:'r180',5:'r180lr',6:'r270',7:'r270lr'}
def variants(b):
    res=[]
    for k in range(4):
        r=np.rot90(b,k); res.append((names[2*k],r)); res.append((names[2*k+1],np.fliplr(r)))
    return res
center=(9,10)
for u in range(-2,3):
    for v in range(-3,4):
        # block rows offsets 3u-1..3u+1
        rr=center[0]+3*u-1; cc=center[1]+3*v-1
        if rr<0 or cc<0 or rr+3>19 or cc+3>19: continue
        blk=out[rr:rr+3,cc:cc+3]
        if np.all(blk==8):
            print(f"(u={u},v={v}) EMPTY"); continue
        matches=[]
        for (i,j),fb in blocksF.items():
            if i==1 and j==1: continue
            for nm,var in variants(fb):
                if np.array_equal(var,blk): matches.append((i,j,nm))
        print(f"(u={u},v={v}) matches={matches}")

[TOOL CALL run_python]
import numpy as np
from collections import Counter

def solve(inp):
    inp=np.array(inp)
    H,W=inp.shape
    bg=8
    nz=np.argwhere(inp!=bg)
    r0,c0=nz.min(0)
    F=inp[r0:r0+9,c0:c0+9].copy()
    figbg=Counter(F.flatten().tolist()).most_common(1)[0][0]
    cr,cc=r0+4,c0+4
    out=np.full((H,W),bg,int)
    ir_map=[2,1,0]
    for r in range(H):
        for c in range(W):
            dr=r-cr; dc=c-cc
            u=(dr+1)//3; v=(dc+1)//3
            a=dr-(3*u-1); b=dc-(3*v-1)
            if abs(u)>2 or abs(v)>2 or not(u==0 or v==0 or abs(u)==abs(v)):
                out[r][c]=bg
            elif u==0 and v==0:
                if a==1 and b==1:
                    out[r][c]=figbg
                else:
                    ii=ir_map[a]; jj=ir_map[b]
                    out[r][c]=F[3*a+ii][3*b+jj]
            else:
                i=(u+1)%3; j=(v+1)%3
                out[r][c]=F[3*i+a][3*j+b]
    return out

for idx,ex in enumerate(train):
    pred=solve(ex['input']); exp=np.array(ex['output'])
    print("ex",idx,"match",np.array_equal(pred,exp))
    if not np.array_equal(pred,exp):
        diff=np.argwhere(pred!=exp)
        print("num diff",len(diff))
        print(diff[:20])
